_source line 4535 · 2026-05-23T07:02:07.233Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Problem! Reconcile detected exp-016's old PID (293725) as exited with EXIT_CODE=129 and marked it DONE(failed)! But exp-016 was restarted with a new PID (316023). The old cron was watching the old PID.

Now exp-016 is marked DONE/failed in registry, but the actual new process (PID 316023) is still running on cuda:2. I need to fix this - update the registry back and register a new cron for the new PID.

But DONE → RUNNING is not allowed. 

This is the same issue I hit with exp-001. Let me check the state machine again - I see that DONE can transition to PREPARING, so I can go DONE → PREPARING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING to get back to a valid state. Then I'll update the notes and register a new cron for the current PID.

Actually, the worker's submit_training_job might have already created its own cron entry, so I should just fix the registry state directly.
```

[Director 指令] **exp-017 立即切 GPU 模式**

CPU 跑 800 fits 预计 48h+，远超 DDL。立即：

1. Kill 当前 CPU 进程
2. 修改脚本：移除 `JAX_PLATFORMS="cpu"`，改为 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0`
3. 保持 `numpyro.set_host_device_count(4)` 但改为 GPU chains 并行
4. 在 autodl cuda:0 重新提交

这是 3.0→3.5 关键路径实验，不能等。

其余空闲 GPU 暂时不分配新任务——当前瓶颈是论文写作不是实验。

````json
{
  "task_type": "ssh:exp017_switch_to_gpu",
  "project": "gated_mfrm_judge_bias",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：exp-017 从 CPU 切换到 GPU 模式并重新提交\n\n### 服务器\nautodl-8gpu, root\n\n### 步骤\n\n**Step 1: Kill 当前 CPU 进程（ssh_execute）**\n```bash\ntmux kill-session -t exp-017 2>/dev/null\npkill -f run_exp017 2>/dev/null\nsleep 3\n```\n\n**Step 2: 读取当前脚本（ssh_read_only）**\n```bash\ncat /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017/run_exp017.py\n```\n\n**Step 3: 修改脚本切换到 GPU 模式（ssh_execute）**\n\n需要修改的关键点：\n1. 移除或注释掉 `os.environ[\"JAX_PLATFORMS\"] = \"cpu\"` 或类似设置\n2. 设置 `os.environ[\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\"] = \"0\"`（必须在 import jax 之前）\n3. 保持 `numpyro.set_host_device_count(4)` 用于 GPU 上 chains 并行（JAX 支持 GPU 多链并行）\n4. 如果有 `jax.config.update` 相关的 CPU 设置也要移除\n\n用 sed 或 python 修改文件。\n\n**Step 4: 保留已完成的结果（如果脚本支持断点续跑）**\n```bash\n# 检查已完成多少\nwc -l /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017/results/coverage_simulation.csv 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_RESULTS_YET\"\n```\n如果有部分结果且脚本支持续跑，保留结果文件。否则清空重跑。\n\n**Step 5: 验证修改（ssh_read_only）**\n```bash\ngrep -n \"JAX_PLATFORMS\\|CUDA_VISIBLE\\|cpu\\|host_device\" /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017/run_exp017.py\n```\n确认：\n- 无 `JAX_PLATFORMS=\"cpu\"`\n- 有 `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=\"0\"`\n- 有 `set_host_device_count`\n\n**Step 6: 用 submit_training_job 提交到 cuda:0（ssh_execute via tool）**\n```\nexp_id: exp-017\nserver: autodl-8gpu\ngpu_index: 0\ncommand: source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017 && python run_exp017.py\n```\n\n**Step 7: 验证 GPU 启动（ssh_read_only）**\n```bash\nsleep 15\ntail -10 ~/runs/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp-017.log\nnvidia-smi -i 0 --query-compute-apps=pid,process_name,used_memory --format=csv,noheader\n```\n确认进程在 GPU 0 上运行，显存有占用。\n\n### 产出\n1. 修改前后的关键行 diff\n2. 确认 GPU 模式启动成功（PID、显存占用）\n3. 初始日志（确认 MCMC 在 GPU 上跑）",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "registry"
  ],
  "tools": [
    "submit_training_job"
  ]
}
````

```json
{
```

````json
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：检查 exp-015 和 exp-016 进度\n\n服务器：autodl-8gpu, root。全部 ssh_read_only。\n\n### exp-015 (cuda:1, synthetic N=8, PID 291457)\n```bash\nps -p 291457 -o pid,etime 2>/dev/null || echo \"exp-015 DONE\"\nwc -l /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp015/results/synthetic_n8.csv 2>/dev/null\ntail -15 ~/runs/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp-015.log 2>/dev/null\n```\n\n如果已完成（进程不在），读取完整结果：\n```bash\ncat /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp015/results/synthetic_n8.csv\n```\n\n### exp-016 (cuda:2, prior sensitivity, PID 316023)\n```bash\nps -p 316023 -o pid,etime 2>/dev/null || echo \"exp-016 DONE\"\ntail -15 ~/runs/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp-016.log 2>/dev/null\ncat /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp016/results/prior_sensitivity.csv 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_CSV_YET\"\n```\n\n### GPU 状态\n```bash\nnvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,utilization.gpu --format=csv,noheader\n```\n\n### 产出\n- exp-015: 完成率（x/20 seeds）或完整结果\n- exp-016: 当前 prior 和进度\n- GPU 状态总览",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ]
}
````

### Assistant

_source line 4585 · 2026-05-23T07:04:50.378Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
The old exp-017 cron is still monitoring PID 314680 (the killed CPU process). It's escalating because the process is gone. I need to register a new cron for the new PID 318073. But first, the old cron needs to be removed.

The cron system doesn't have a direct delete function in MCP. The reconcile detected it's gone but escalated rather than cleaning up. Let me wait for the switch worker to complete - submit_training_job should have auto-registered a new cron for PID 318073.

Actually, the reconcile says "miss 2/5" which means it's going to keep checking. 

I'll attempt to register a new cron with the updated PID anyway—if the old one blocks the registration, I'll just let it expire naturally rather than getting stuck waiting for cleanup.
```

</details>

````json
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：紧急排查 exp-017 进程退出原因\n\n服务器：autodl-8gpu, root。优先 ssh_read_only。\n\n### 步骤\n\n**1. 检查进程状态**\n```bash\ntmux list-sessions 2>/dev/null | grep exp-017\nps aux | grep run_exp017 | grep -v grep\nnvidia-smi -i 0 --query-compute-apps=pid,process_name,used_memory --format=csv,noheader 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_GPU0_PROCESS\"\n```\n\n**2. 读日志尾部（关键）**\n```bash\ntail -80 ~/runs/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp-017.log\n```\n\n**3. 检查结果文件**\n```bash\nls -la /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017/results/\nwc -l /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017/results/coverage_simulation.csv 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_CSV\"\ncat /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017/results/coverage_simulation.csv 2>/dev/null | head -20\ncat /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017/results/coverage_summary.csv 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_SUMMARY\"\n```\n\n**4. 检查是否有 Python traceback 或 OOM**\n```bash\ngrep -i \"error\\|traceback\\|exception\\|oom\\|killed\\|segfault\" ~/runs/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp-017.log | tail -20\n```\n\n**5. 检查 tmux session 内容（如果 session 还在）**\n```bash\ntmux capture-pane -t exp-017 -p 2>/dev/null | tail -30 || echo \"NO_TMUX_SESSION\"\n```\n\n### 产出\n1. 进程是否仍在运行\n2. 如果退出：退出原因（成功完成/报错/OOM/killed）\n3. 已完成的结果数量和内容\n4. 如果失败：具体错误信息和建议修复方案",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ]
}
````

_source line 4667 · 2026-05-23T07:11:53.400Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
There's no dedicated "delete_cron" MCP tool in the agent-ml-research toolset. The reconcile said "miss 4/5" - one more miss and it should auto-clean. Let me try polling it manually - poll_cron will detect the process is gone and since it's at miss 4/5, one more poll should trigger cleanup.

Actually, looking at the poll_cron description: "Process exited + no W&B → DONE(failed)". If I poll it, it'll mark exp-017 as DONE(failed) which I don't want since the experiment is actually running with a new PID.
 
The registry already shows exp-017 as RUNNING with the new PID from submit_training_job, so when the cron hits miss 5/5 and auto-cleans, it might conflict with that updated state. I'll trigger one more reconcile to push it to the cleanup threshold and see what happens.
```

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user operates as Director of the gated_mfrm_judge_bias ML research project (G-MFRM: Gated Multi-Facet Rasch Model for LLM judge bias and benchmark contamination correction). **Deadline is May 26, 2026 (EMNLP 2026 ARR submission, 3 days away).** The AI agent manages experiments across two servers: rtx6000-4x (4×RTX PRO 6000 96GB) and autodl-8gpu (4×RTX PRO 6000 96GB). Key requests in this session:
   - Investigate exp-001 false failure from server restart recovery
   - Fix exp-001 registry (conclusion→success, notes about false failure)
   - Confirm autodl cuda:2 W7 rerun terminated
   - Clean up exp-004 ALLOCATED/success anomalous state
   - Run exp-013 R1-W6 Stage 2 Weight Sensitivity (4 weight configs)
   - Launch 3 new experiments: exp-014 (exhaustive judge subsampling), exp-015 (synthetic N=8), exp-016 (prior sensitivity)
   - Emergency MCMC parameter correction: all must use chains=4, warmup=2000, samples=4000
   - Kill vLLM on rtx cuda:1+2
   - Kill exp-014 (120h >> DDL)
   - Launch exp-017 Murphy-Topel Coverage Simulation
   - Switch exp-017 from CPU to GPU mode
   - Adjust exp-016 params (reduced for non-HalfNormal priors)
   - Delete stale exp-017 cron monitoring dead CPU PID
   - Multiple urgent status checks on all running experiments

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - G-MFRM: logit(P(Y≥k)) = θ_n - b_i - d_j - τ_k + c_n·e_i (three-stage estimation)
   - NumPyro/JAX MCMC with NUTS sampler, HalfNormal hierarchical prior
   - MCMC parameters: chains=4, warmup=2000, samples=4000, target_accept=0.95 (mandatory for valid c_n estimation; chains=2/samples=2000 causes c_n compression)
   - Kendall τ for ranking agreement with Chatbot Arena Elo
   - Murphy-Topel asymptotic CI correction and coverage simulation
   - Stage 2 exposure estimation weights (reference-anchoring vs cross-model-agreement)
   - Prior sensitivity: HalfNormal, Exponential, LogNormal, Gamma for c_n
   - Registry state machine: PREPARING → PENDING → QUEUED → ALLOCATED → RUNNING → ANALYZING → DONE (forward only, DONE can go to PREPARING for retry)
   - Cron monitoring system: register_cron monitors PID, auto-cleans after 5 consecutive misses
   - JAX_PLATFORMS="cpu" forces CPU-only MCMC (numpyro.set_host_device_count(4) for CPU chain parallelism)
   - submit_training_job auto-registers cron and creates tmux session

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **Registry (via MCP tools)**: Central truth source for all experiments. 17 experiments total (exp-001 through exp-017, mvp_001-003, exp-w7-ablation). Registry updates require specific field sequences for state transitions.
   - **`/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp013/results/weight_sensitivity.csv`** (rtx6000-4x): Weight sensitivity results showing τ=0.7857 identical across all 4 configs. Confirms R1-W6 issue resolved.
   - **`/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp014/run_exp014.py`** (autodl-8gpu): Exhaustive C(9,k) subsampling script. Originally with wrong MCMC params (chains=2, samples=1000), fixed to chains=4/warmup=2000/samples=4000, then entire experiment killed (120h >> DDL).
   - **`/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp015/results/synthetic_n8.csv`** (autodl-8gpu): Synthetic N=8 calibration results. 14 rows (7 seeds × 2 methods). Joint G-MFRM: θ RMSE 0.084 (2x better), c RMSE 0.520 (2.8x better) vs sequential.
   - **`/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp016/run_exp016.py`** (autodl-8gpu): Prior sensitivity script, modified to use different MCMC params per prior: HalfNormal gets chains=4/warmup=2000/samples=4000, others get chains=2/warmup=1000/samples=2000.
   - **`/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017/run_exp017.py`** (autodl-8gpu): Murphy-Topel coverage simulation. Originally hardcoded `JAX_PLATFORMS="cpu"`, modified to `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES="0"` for GPU mode. N∈{8,20,50,100}×200 seeds, MCMC chains=4/warmup=2000/samples=4000. Supports resume (dedup by N+seed).
   - **`/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp017/results/coverage_simulation.csv`**: 8 lines (header + 7 completed iterations)
   - **`/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp001/results/arena_hard_judgments/`** (rtx6000-4x): Llama judge collection data. llama-3.3-70b: 6,061 lines + shard2: 5,821 lines = 11,882 total. Collection complete.
   - **report.md, ROADMAP.md, memory.md**: Project knowledge files managed via MCP tools.

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **Registry DONE→RUNNING rejected**: States can only go forward. Fix: transition through DONE→PREPARING→QUEUED→ALLOCATED→RUNNING (5 sequential updates).
   - **conclusion="pending" rejected**: Only valid values are failed/killed/negative/success. Had to leave exp-001 as conclusion=failed initially until Director ordered it changed to success.
   - **Eval experiment missing spec fields**: Experiments with `parent` field require `checkpoint_source` and `eval_data` in spec for RUNNING transition. Fix: include those fields in the spec parameter.
   - **exp-014 wrong MCMC params**: Worker submitted with chains=2/samples=1000. Director caught it. Fix: spawned new worker to kill process, modify script (sed), clear partial results, resubmit with chains=4/warmup=2000/samples=4000.
   - **exp-016 false failure from reconcile_crons**: Old cron monitored dead PID 293725 (killed during restart). reconcile_crons detected exit code 129 → marked DONE(failed). Fix: DONE→PREPARING→...→RUNNING state recovery, register new cron for PID 316023.
   - **exp-017 CPU mode**: Worker hardcoded `JAX_PLATFORMS="cpu"` in script, so GPU was unused. Fix: Director ordered switch to GPU, worker modified script to use CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES="0".
   - **exp-017 cron nightmare**: Old cron monitoring dead CPU PID 314680 kept firing false alarms. No MCP delete_cron tool available. Fix: waited for miss 5/5 auto-cleanup via reconcile_crons, then fixed the resulting false DONE(failed) marking. Registry recovery again: DONE→PREPARING→QUEUED→ALLOCATED→(RUNNING pending).
   - **Worker submitted to wrong server**: exp-013 worker submitted to rtx6000-4x instead of Director-specified autodl-8gpu. Not critical since data existed on both servers.
   - **cuda:3 occupied by test_dry.py**: exp-017 worker's dry-run tests left processes consuming 97GB. Fix: killed via separate worker.
   - **register_cron "already registered" error**: Can't overwrite existing cron. Must delete first (no delete tool) or wait for auto-cleanup.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **exp-001 recovery**: Confirmed Llama collection still running (52%), registered cron, updated notes. Later confirmed collection complete (11,882 lines). vLLM eventually killed (Director approved).
   - **exp-013 weight sensitivity**: Successfully demonstrated complete insensitivity of downstream τ to Stage 2 weight choice (all 4 configs: τ=0.7857). R1-W6 reviewer issue closed.
   - **MCMC parameter standardization**: Enforced chains=4/warmup=2000/samples=4000 across all experiments after Director flagged c_n compression from reduced params.
   - **exp-014 killed**: 247 subsets × 30min = 120h, far exceeding DDL. 10-random results from exp-006 sufficient.
   - **exp-017 CPU→GPU migration**: Complex multi-step process involving kill, script modification, resubmission, and repeated cron false alarm handling.
   - **exp-015 intermediate results**: Very positive — joint G-MFRM consistently outperforms sequential on N=8 synthetic data (θ RMSE 2x better, c RMSE 2.8x better), validating that non-significant bootstrap on real data is power issue.

GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",

```json
      "statusline-setup"
    ],
    "addedLines": [
      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"
    ],
    "removedTypes": [],

  },
```

```text
Now I'm thinking about how to use the idle GPUs. Looking at the priority queue, exp-002 (item exposure estimation) is P0 with no dependencies on the running experiments—it just needs the exp-001 data I already have. exp-003 (G-MFRM real data fit) is also P0 but depends on exp-002 finishing first.

The real constraint is that exp-002 requires reference benchmark variants, which is a whole pipeline to set up. With the May 26 deadline just three days away, the running experiments (exp-015/016/017) are synthetic validation work that supports existing claims, while the real data pipeline (exp-001→002→003→004) is the critical path.

I should report this resource situation to Director and let them decide what to prioritize, but in the meantime I can spawn workers to check on exp-015/016/017 progress and handle any CPU-bound tasks in parallel.

Let me also check the report.md file to see what the latest handover state is.
```

Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

````json
  "task_prompt": "紧急检查 exp-016 和 exp-015 的状态。\n\n服务器：autodl-8gpu\n项目路径：/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/\n\n## 任务 1：exp-016 Prior Sensitivity（PID 316023, cuda:2）\n\n1. 检查进程是否还在：\n   ```\n   ps -p 316023 -o pid,state,etime,pcpu,rss,args\n   ```\n\n2. 检查结果文件：\n   ```\n   ls -la /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp016/results/\n   ```\n\n3. 如果有 CSV 输出，读取完整内容：\n   ```\n   cat /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp016/results/*.csv\n   ```\n   我们需要看到 4 个 prior（HalfNormal, Exponential, LogNormal, Gamma）的：\n   - Kendall τ（与 Arena 的 ranking agreement）\n   - c_n ranking（Spearman ρ between priors）\n   - 任何收敛诊断（R-hat, ESS）\n\n4. 检查日志获取更多上下文：\n   ```\n   cat /root/runs/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp-016.log 2>/dev/null | tail -30\n   ```\n\n5. 如果进程已退出，检查退出码：\n   ```\n   tmux list-sessions 2>/dev/null | grep -i 016\n   ```\n\n## 任务 2：exp-015 Synthetic N=8（实际 python PID 291466, cuda:1）\n\n1. 检查进度：\n   ```\n   ps -p 291466 -o pid,state,etime,pcpu,rss\n   wc -l /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp015/results/synthetic_n8.csv\n   tail -5 /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp015/results/synthetic_n8.csv\n   ```\n\n2. 检查日志：\n   ```\n   cat /root/runs/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp-015.log 2>/dev/null | tail -10\n   ```\n\n请完整输出所有原始数据，不要省略。",
````

[Director 指令] **rtx6000-4x cuda:1/2 未注册进程 — 第三次提醒，必须处理**

nvidia-smi 显示 rtx6000-4x cuda:1 和 cuda:2 利用率 100% 但 registry 无对应实验。Reviewer 上次诊断为 "被 kill 的 vLLM 进程留下的 driver residual state"。

立即执行：
1. SSH 到 rtx6000-4x
2. `nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps=pid,gpu_uuid,used_memory --format=csv` 检查是否有实际进程
3. 如果无进程但 util=100%，执行 `nvidia-smi --gpu-reset -i 1,2` 重置
4. 重置后确认 `nvidia-smi` 显示 0% utilization
5. 汇报结果

````json
{
  "task_type": "ssh:rtx_gpu_reset_cuda12",
  "project": "gated_mfrm_judge_bias",
  "task_prompt": "rtx6000-4x 的 cuda:1 和 cuda:2 显示 100% 利用率但 registry 无对应实验，疑似被 kill 的 vLLM 残留 driver state。Director 第三次要求处理。\n\n服务器：rtx6000-4x\n项目路径：/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/\n\n请按以下步骤执行：\n\n1. 检查所有 GPU 上是否有实际计算进程：\n   ```\n   nvidia-smi --query-compute-apps=pid,gpu_uuid,used_memory --format=csv\n   nvidia-smi\n   ```\n\n2. 检查 cuda:1 和 cuda:2 是否有实际运行的进程（列出所有 GPU 进程）：\n   ```\n   nvidia-smi -i 1 --query-compute-apps=pid,process_name,used_memory --format=csv\n   nvidia-smi -i 2 --query-compute-apps=pid,process_name,used_memory --format=csv\n   ```\n\n3. 如果有实际进程占用，kill 它们：\n   ```\n   # 列出并 kill\n   ```\n\n4. 如果无进程但 utilization 仍然 100%，执行 GPU 重置：\n   ```\n   nvidia-smi --gpu-reset -i 1,2\n   ```\n   注意：如果 gpu-reset 报错 \"not supported\" 或需要 root，尝试：\n   ```\n   sudo nvidia-smi --gpu-reset -i 1,2\n   ```\n\n5. 重置后确认状态：\n   ```\n   nvidia-smi\n   ```\n   确认 cuda:1 和 cuda:2 的 utilization 显示 0%，无残留进程。\n\n6. 报告完整的 nvidia-smi 输出（重置前后对比）。",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ]
}
````

```json
  "content": "[Director 指令] **2 个新实验 — 立即启动**\n\n### exp-018: N=20 Synthetic Δτ Calibration (P0, autodl cuda:2)\n\n**假设**: N=20 时 G-MFRM vs sequential 的 Δτ 达到统计显著（论文 power analysis 预测 N≈17 for 80% power）。这经验性验证了 power analysis。\n\n**设计**: 与 exp-015 完全相同的代码框架，只改 N_models=20：\n- 合成数据: N=20 models, I=176 items, J=9 judges\n- 20 seeds\n- 每 seed: fit sequential + fit joint G-MFRM\n- MCMC: chains=4, warmup=2000, samples=4000\n- 报告: per-seed θ_RMSE, θ_τ, c_RMSE + paired Wilcoxon test on Δτ\n- **关键指标**: Δτ 是否 p<0.05\n\n**代码**: 直接复制 exp-015 脚本，改 `n_models=20`。合成数据生成时 ground truth θ 和 c_n 从与 N=8 相同的分布采样（只是更多 models）。\n\n**服务器**: autodl-8gpu, cuda:2\n**预计时长**: ~7h (N=20 MCMC 比 N=8 慢 ~2.5x)\n\n### exp-019: MFRM-only θ 差异表 (P1, autodl cuda:3)\n\n**目的**: 生成 per-model Δθ 表格（G-MFRM θ vs MFRM-only θ），可视化 contamination bias 如何被吸收进 ability estimates。\n\n**设计**:\n1. 先检查 exp-003b 输出目录是否已有 MFRM-only 的 θ 估计（Stage 1 MFRM fit 的输出）\n2. 如果有 → 直接提取，与 G-MFRM θ 对比，生成 Δθ 表格\n3. 如果没有 → 在真实数据上跑一次 MFRM-only MCMC fit（~30min）\n4. 输出: 8 个 model 的 (θ_MFRM, θ_GMFRM, Δθ, c_n) 表格\n\n**服务器**: autodl-8gpu, cuda:3\n**预计时长**: 30min（如果需要 refit）或 5min（如果数据已有）\n\n两个实验独立，可并行启动。先注册 registry 再提交。"
```

_source line 4887 · 2026-05-23T08:53:26.840Z_

[Director 指令] **2 个新实验 — 立即启动**

### exp-018: N=20 Synthetic Δτ Calibration (P0, autodl cuda:2)

**假设**: N=20 时 G-MFRM vs sequential 的 Δτ 达到统计显著（论文 power analysis 预测 N≈17 for 80% power）。这经验性验证了 power analysis。

**设计**: 与 exp-015 完全相同的代码框架，只改 N_models=20：
- 合成数据: N=20 models, I=176 items, J=9 judges
- 20 seeds
- 每 seed: fit sequential + fit joint G-MFRM
- MCMC: chains=4, warmup=2000, samples=4000
- 报告: per-seed θ_RMSE, θ_τ, c_RMSE + paired Wilcoxon test on Δτ
- **关键指标**: Δτ 是否 p<0.05

**代码**: 直接复制 exp-015 脚本，改 `n_models=20`。合成数据生成时 ground truth θ 和 c_n 从与 N=8 相同的分布采样（只是更多 models）。

**服务器**: autodl-8gpu, cuda:2
**预计时长**: ~7h (N=20 MCMC 比 N=8 慢 ~2.5x)

### exp-019: MFRM-only θ 差异表 (P1, autodl cuda:3)

**目的**: 生成 per-model Δθ 表格（G-MFRM θ vs MFRM-only θ），可视化 contamination bias 如何被吸收进 ability estimates。

**设计**:
1. 先检查 exp-003b 输出目录是否已有 MFRM-only 的 θ 估计（Stage 1 MFRM fit 的输出）
2. 如果有 → 直接提取，与 G-MFRM θ 对比，生成 Δθ 表格
3. 如果没有 → 在真实数据上跑一次 MFRM-only MCMC fit（~30min）
4. 输出: 8 个 model 的 (θ_MFRM, θ_GMFRM, Δθ, c_n) 表格

**服务器**: autodl-8gpu, cuda:3
**预计时长**: 30min（如果需要 refit）或 5min（如果数据已有）

两个实验独立，可并行启动。先注册 registry 再提交。

````json
{
  "task_type": "ssh:exp018_synthetic_n20",
  "project": "gated_mfrm_judge_bias",
  "task_prompt": "创建并提交 exp-018: N=20 Synthetic Δτ Calibration 实验。\n\n服务器：autodl-8gpu\nGPU：cuda:2\n项目路径：/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/\n\n## 任务\n\n1. 复制 exp-015 的脚本作为基础：\n   ```\n   mkdir -p /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp018/results\n   cp /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp015/run_exp015.py /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp018/run_exp018.py\n   ```\n\n2. 修改 run_exp018.py：\n   - 改 n_models = 20（从 8 改为 20）\n   - 改 结果输出路径为 exp018/results/synthetic_n20.csv\n   - 保持其他参数不变：I=176 items, J=9 judges, 20 seeds\n   - MCMC: chains=4, warmup=2000, samples=4000, target_accept=0.95\n   - 每 seed: fit sequential + fit joint G-MFRM\n   - 输出列：seed, method, theta_RMSE, theta_tau, c_RMSE, c_pearson, elapsed, rhat_max, ess_min\n   - 加 paired Wilcoxon test 汇总（p-value for Δτ across seeds）\n   - 确保支持 resume（检查已完成的 seed 跳过）\n\n3. 验证脚本能启动：\n   ```\n   cd /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp018\n   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n   python run_exp018.py --help 2>/dev/null || python -c \"import run_exp018; print('OK')\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"检查脚本...\"\n   ```\n\n4. 用 submit_training_job 提交（不要用 nohup）：\n   - exp_id: exp-018\n   - server: autodl-8gpu\n   - gpu_ids: [2]\n   - command: source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp018 && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2 python run_exp018.py\n   - project: gated_mfrm_judge_bias\n\n5. 提交后更新 registry 的 spec（补充 data_path, data_count, model_name, seed 等 provenance 信息）。\n\n**关键**：合成数据的 ground truth θ 和 c_n 分布必须与 exp-015 N=8 相同（只是 N 从 8 变成 20），确保结果可比。检查 exp-015 代码中 θ 和 c 的生成分布，在 exp-018 中保持一致。",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "registry"
  ]
}
````

````json
{
  "task_type": "ssh:exp019_mfrm_delta_theta",
  "project": "gated_mfrm_judge_bias",
  "task_prompt": "创建并提交 exp-019: MFRM-only θ 差异表实验。\n\n服务器：autodl-8gpu\nGPU：cuda:3\n项目路径：/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/\n\n## 任务\n\n目的：生成 per-model Δθ 表格（G-MFRM θ vs MFRM-only θ），可视化 contamination bias 如何被吸收进 ability estimates。\n\n### Step 1: 检查 exp-003b 是否已有 MFRM-only θ 输出\n\n```\nls -la /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp003b/results/\n# 查找 Stage 1 MFRM fit 的 θ 估计文件\nfind /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp003b/ -name \"*.csv\" -o -name \"*.json\" -o -name \"*.npy\" | head -20\n# 检查是否有 mfrm_only 或 stage1 相关文件\nfind /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/ -path \"*/exp003b/*\" -name \"*mfrm*\" -o -path \"*/exp003b/*\" -name \"*stage1*\" -o -path \"*/exp003b/*\" -name \"*theta*\" | head -20\n```\n\n### Step 2: 根据结果分支\n\n**如果 MFRM-only θ 已存在**：\n- 提取 MFRM-only θ 和 G-MFRM θ\n- 同时提取 c_n（contamination exposure）\n- 生成表格：model_name, θ_MFRM, θ_GMFRM, Δθ (= θ_MFRM - θ_GMFRM), c_n\n- 保存到 /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp019/results/delta_theta.csv\n\n**如果 MFRM-only θ 不存在**：\n- 在真实数据上跑一次 MFRM-only MCMC fit\n- 使用 exp-003b 的数据和代码框架，但只跑 Stage 1（无 contamination gating）\n- MCMC: chains=4, warmup=2000, samples=4000\n- 然后提取 θ 与 G-MFRM θ 对比\n\n### Step 3: 创建脚本\n\n```\nmkdir -p /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp019/results\n```\n\n写 run_exp019.py 脚本，功能：\n1. 加载 exp-003b 的数据（judgments + exposure scores）\n2. 如果需要 MFRM-only refit：用 NumPyro 跑标准 MFRM（无 c_n·e_i 项）\n3. 加载 G-MFRM θ（从 exp-003b 结果）\n4. 输出 CSV: model_name, theta_mfrm, theta_gmfrm, delta_theta, c_n\n5. 打印汇总统计（Δθ 与 c_n 的 Spearman 相关）\n\n### Step 4: 提交\n\n用 submit_training_job 提交：\n- exp_id: exp-019\n- server: autodl-8gpu\n- gpu_ids: [3]\n- command: source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp019 && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 python run_exp019.py\n- project: gated_mfrm_judge_bias\n\n### Step 5: 更新 registry spec\n\n补充 provenance 信息（data_path, data_count, model_name, checkpoint_source 等）。\n\n**注意**：exp-003b 的输出目录结构需要先探查，不要假设文件名。",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "registry"
  ]
}
````
